# Historical vocabulary owner review — revision 15

This preserves the review IDs used for the owner’s decisions. The final 372-word
review approves 325 spellings and reserves 47 as bonus-only, effective in revision
16 together with the 53 all-play exclusions. See
[the revision-16 list](VOCABULARY_OWNER_REVIEW_V16.md) for current eligibility.
Earlier counts and pending notes below describe the review stages before that decision.

- **819 required-eligible words:** 680 used in the campaign and 139 unused.
- **443 additional bonus-only words:** including valid plurals and words not approved
  as required answers. Together these make **1,262 currently recognized spellings**.
- **10 words approved for a future rebuild** appear separately at the end. They are
  already among the 443 bonus-only words, not ten additional spellings.
- The reference dictionary retains 50 excluded spellings for historical purposes;
  they are not playable in the current revision and are omitted from the tables.

Use `keep`, `bonus-only` or `exclude`, with any spelling/meaning correction. Reply with
words or review IDs. Required-word IDs 1–819 preserve the previous review numbering;
bonus IDs begin with B. These IDs differ from the recent 135-candidate proposal.

Meanings are the recorded dictionary senses, provided for review rather than as a
claim that every entry is suitable. The owner previously approved 140 retained
words; the other 679 required approvals came from editorial review and remain open
to owner correction. All words may be reconsidered in this review.

## Latest owner decisions — 53 exclusions pending revision 16

The owner rejected the 53 spellings below from required answers, accepted variants
and bonuses. Their rows retain the original review IDs and are marked `Exclude —
owner decision; effective from revision 16`. The raw v15 allowlist and campaign
remain unchanged while the full review continues; the exclusion policy is saved
for revision 16 and later. A future release needs a rebuilt campaign, review and
save migration before these removals take effect in the app.

After these decisions, **766 of the existing 819 required approvals remain**.
Including the ten previously approved additions awaiting a rebuild gives **776**.
The 135-candidate proposal remains pending. Of the rejected words, 50 currently
occupy 104 required slots across 77 boards; three are currently unused as targets.
These are individual decisions, not a blanket ban on every numeral or adverb.

Validation of the pending policy: `npm run check` passed, including solving all
240 current levels. All 125 Python tests, Ruff checks and mypy passed; the iOS
export succeeded. Regression checks cover all 53 exclusions from revision 16,
preservation of revision-15 eligibility, and continued bonus recognition without
automatically approving new required words. Existing pack, allowlist and reference
dictionary files were verified unchanged. This is not a rebuilt revision-16
campaign or a native-device test.

ათასი, ათი, ასე, ასი, აქეთ, აქვე, ახლა, ახლოს, ახლო, ბლოგი, გარე, დღეს, ერთი, ექვსი, ზევით, ზემოთ, ისე, ისევ, იქვე, იქით, კარგი, კევი, კვლავ, კიდევ, მაღლა, მაშინ, მერე, მეტად, ნელა, ოდნავ, ოთხი, ორი, ორმოცი, ოცი, რვა, სამი, სამოცი, სულ, უკვე, უფრო, ქვედა, ქვევით, ქვემოთ, შვიდი, შიგ, შიდა, შინ, ჩქარა, ცუდად, ცხრა, წინ, ხუთი, ჯერ.

ბალონი, ბაჟე, გარაჟი, ზუსტად, კარგად, კასეტი, უცებ, ფანარი, შიგნით, ძალიან, ხშირად, ჯიპი

existing bonus vocabulary to required eligibility. No such promotion has occurred.
Bonus-board counts describe the current 240 boards; zero means no current board
lists that word as a bonus. Bonus words never block completion.

## Filtered bonus review — 372 words

This is the full subset requested by the owner, not a claim that all 372 words are
suitable as required answers. The filter selects current bonus-only spellings with
3–6 letters and removes recorded plural and all-play exclusions, including the 53
pending exclusions. It does not replace individual spelling, grammar, standard-use,
meaning and familiarity review, or prove that every word can fit campaign boards.

**Correction to the earlier explanation:** this count includes three words already
explicitly reserved for bonuses (`თელა`, `რაზა`, `ურცი`) and ten previously approved
words awaiting the next rebuild. Thus 359 entries remain for potential new target
approval; the three explicit bonus-only decisions remain in force.

Review IDs **C001–C372** apply only to this filtered list and are distinct from the
1–819 required-word IDs, B-prefixed full bonus IDs and the earlier 135-candidate
proposal. Use `Cnnn keep` for a proposed required answer, `Cnnn bonus-only`, or
`Cnnn exclude` for exclusion from all play. Previously recorded exclusions and
approvals are preserved until explicitly changed. No words are promoted by this list.
